## Implemented systems and preserved behavior

### Commerce, layout and accessibility

Native prices, stock, variations, product authority and Woo hooks remain
authoritative. The cart now preserves line subtotal, filtered
unit/subtotal/quantity/remove hooks, sold-individually limits, backorder
notices, after-name/coupon hooks, native notices and usable touch/focus states.
Native error notices retain urgent insertion behavior and escaping with valid
inner list semantics. Checkout, guest Account/reset and content-route
padding/token/reflow problems were repaired. Long search queries wrap without
changing native search grouping. Bag fragments, Quick View, variation selection,
product imagery, lightbox, fit-dialog focus and native navigation remain intact.
No payment or order was submitted.

The authoritative feature-by-feature record is
`tasks/v2-completion-20260906/feature-completion.md`; detailed journeys and
limitations are in `journey-review.md` and `skyy-completion.md`.

### CSS and package cleanup

- Partitioned 105 owned CSS rules (13,332 bytes) with original declaration/order
  and mixed-selector handling preserved.
- Generated native archive projection `archive-theme.min.css`: 29,883 bytes
  versus complete `theme.min.css` 54,185 bytes. Full source/full min remain.
  Projection SHA-256:
  `3d4ba06af5a3c1df60995b8d398fbb08ba9b4219c53a53eaf4b001ceaf29123f`.
- A strict head/main-archive policy allows Core to inline seven verified styles
  within 100,000 bytes. Exact hashes, URLs, paths, dependencies, metadata,
  queue/resolved order and filter inventory gate eligibility. Unknown plugin
  paths/filter/media/source/order contexts fall back; late rollback removes only
  owned chunks while preserving extension changes. Known native common.css is
  allowed only if the original 40 KB allocation already inlined it. Opt-outs:
  `skyyrose2_archive_theme_projection_enabled`,
  `skyyrose2_archive_inline_enabled`.
- **No Home projection or Home expanded inline policy shipped.** The archive
  policy must not be copied to Home without new evidence.
- Archived two unreachable drafts (8,907 bytes) with exact source retained.
  Removed one impossible House-motion enqueue; kept the module, outputs,
  registry, art and Town Line relationships.
- Excluded two unused authoring MP4s (38,020,556 bytes) from release ZIP only;
  retained originals.
- No native jQuery defer, concatenation, or preload experiment shipped.

### Fonts and responsive frame delivery

- Deterministic Archivo derivative: 90,096 → 34,976 bytes; freeze unused width
  axis at 100, preserve weight axis 100–900, glyph/cmap coverage and license.
  Original remains a native fallback. WordPress theme.json face order prevents
  same-format URL collapse and duplicate download.
- Inter unicode-range now reflects actual supported glyph coverage.
- Source-derived q80 frame variants: four 384px files total 196,922 bytes versus
  q90 279,002; four optional 360px files total 176,088. Original 640px art
  remains. Independent review accepted minor grain softening without
  aperture/alpha/identity loss.
- At DPR1, ≤392px viewport selects 360; 414 selects 384; ≥480 or DPR2
  selects 640. Missing/corrupt optional derivative fails back to 384/640.
  Hash/path/symlink/dimension/WebP checks prevent unverified substitutions.
- The first native archive card receives high priority before Woo increments its
  loop index; later cards remain lazy.

### Hero and Ask Skyy

- Hero publishes the browser-selected `currentSrc` as native poster when the
  image is available, before asynchronous decode continuation. Decode still
  gates film loading. It never substitutes desktop img.src or republishes an
  unchanged URL.
- Tracks activated native source candidates. WebM failure preserves MP4
  fallback; all-source exhaustion pauses the video, clears pending/readiness and
  exposes the original image through the existing 700ms fade.
  Duplicate/preactivation errors are ignored; late play settlement cannot revive
  terminal failure.
- Final controller SHA-256:
  `4d4281b86964740ddb6ae73a635528b37f2a90274d91f7b347ea7d588b37ea93`. Existing
  film bytes, poster authority, CSS transition, pause, reduced motion,
  Save-Data, visibility/pagehide/bfcache behavior preserved.
- Ask Skyy keeps the same 6,058,568-byte GLB and renderer, with stabilized

## Performance — target remains OPEN

Acceptance requires repeatable mobile LCP≤2500ms and CLS≤0.1 for Home/PDP/Shop,
with desktop preserved under the declared matching profile. Do not infer
completion from one pass, average away failures, or change Lighthouse/throttling
to pass.

Final-profile baseline source `aabd2bffdc1e322862acd05a5640c14cf00f3acf` is a
verified588-blob export in `static-baseline/`; binding is
`static-baseline-binding.json`. Baseline and candidate used identical native
snapshot, Nginx config, PHP8.5.6/OPcache profile, exact-origin proxy and
unchanged simulated Lighthouse harness. Both were restarted and ran the
same28HTTP warm/parity sequence (446 cached scripts).

| ------------ | ----------------------: | -----------------------------------: | -------------------------------------- |
| Home mobile  |                    4143 |                                 2717 | FAIL mobile target                     |
| Home desktop |                     734 |                                  773 | Fast; small difference in one lab pair |
| PDP mobile   |                    2443 |                                 2422 | PASS this run                          |
| PDP desktop  |                     657 |                                  614 | PASS this run                          |
| Shop mobile  |                    3165 |                                 2334 | PASS this run                          |

Reports: `lighthouse/lighthouse-final-ipv4-baseline-*.report.json` and
`lighthouse/lighthouse-final-ipv4-candidate-1-*.report.json`.
Accessibility/best-practices scores100 in these candidate cases. Local SEO58
reflects intentional noindex and missing meta description; not production SEO
proof.

Earlier native-static-v1 candidate: Home2191/2216/**2978**; PDP and Shop passed
all three runs. Third Home failure was a late VIDEO poster paint before film
request; corrected early poster code shipped afterward. Do not combine those
results with final IPv4 profile as a single acceptance series.

Final2717ms failure instead identifies **IMG** LCP. Observed TTFB225 + image
delay10 + image9 + render73≈317ms; simulated LCP2717/FCP2113. Film starts
afterward. The early poster race is fixed in that trace; do not keep treating
all current Home failures as video delay.

Full theme offered at100KB does not fit because Core selects eligible sheets by
ascending size. Home10owned sheets total137,377 bytes plus native common4203.
Selective100 offered baseline-inline set plus full theme/Home composition
(98,520 total), leaving controls/global-shell/mascot external; first sample
improved but repeat failed. All150 inlined all10 unchanged files but failed both
repeats. Reject extra complexity absent reliable improvement. Partial
implementation was removed via exact reverse patch; retained only at
`rejected-home-inline/partial-selective100.patch`. No Home source/runtime policy
remains.

Native jQuery-core early preload kept execution/order and existing CSS
unchanged; one sample passed and the repeat failed. Its MU adapter was removed.
No preload implementation shipped. Latest reports still show IMG LCP and native
blocking scripts. **Root cause of residual repeated variability and the next
successful fix are unresolved.** Below-fold content-visibility and deeper
request/paint dependency analysis were only untested thoughts, not chosen
implementation or validated fixes.

The experiments and source/HTML/logs are in completion artifacts.
`performance-delivery-evolution.json` and `manager-verification.md` are older
checkpoints and do not yet include every latest result above; this memory
explicitly supersedes their current-status claims, not their historical raw
measurements. `verification-index.json` and `task-ledger.jsonl` also need final
reconciliation on resume.
